## October 6, 2026: corrected local subscription route

On baseline `78e0f8712f71c5bdf5dbf5ac13f1dcf6514a1a2e`, Windows/Node 24.12.0/pnpm 12.3.4, the owner approved a fresh isolated official-client sign-in and authorized repairing local preparation plus their own subscription. [ADR 062](adr/062-bounded-local-subscription-transport.md) records the transport/schema correction. The previous failed benchmark remains below as historical evidence.

The original broker's independent read-direction timeout was reproduced without an external network using delayed synthetic upstream data and scaled timing: premature closure occurred before the response. The fix measures shared activity and preserves absolute caps. The subscription profile is selected only by the trusted launcher; normal media keeps its existing limits. Unsupported built-in OpenAI provider overrides were removed. The client keeps bounded default transport retries, disabled unbounded retries and one application attempt per source. Protocol exit now fails an active turn immediately, and oversized lines close the client rather than disappearing silently. All timestamps above are October 5 UTC, corresponding to October 6 on the laptop in Europe/Berlin. Managed elapsed times start after upload. They exclude local URL acquisition for those upload copies (38.099 s and 36.029 s) and upload transfer. Thus these completed jobs establish managed processing of uploaded media, not successful independent cloud acquisition of both source URLs. Native turn durations include orchestration; the second worker's measured reporting interval was 84.593 s and excludes initial discovery. Unrun stage times are unknown, not zero.

The managed receipts recorded A 25,697 input / 2,242 output tokens and B 26,811 / 2,684. [Google's non-global standard pricing](https://cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-agent-platform/generative-ai/pricing) of USD 0.33 / million input and USD 2.75 / million output yields approximately USD 0.01465 and USD 0.01623 inference respectively. These are list-price estimates, not invoices or total worker costs. Storage/compute cash cost is not independently attributable. Internal inferenceMicros includes a conservative conversion margin. Own-subscription turns have no separate API price substituted; exact plan allowance consumption remains unknown.

Operator image spot checks confirmed the UI creator attribution in a frame, absent by name from speech. The managed website analysis recovered Alt text and USP bar, both misheard by local Whisper. It covered fourteen website suggestions in two groups, while its summary repeated fifteen without explaining the final creator DM prompt. The managed UI transcript misheard a performance phrase that local Whisper transcribed more clearly. Browser performance, licence permissions, universal applicability and analytics/privacy requirements remain unverified source claims. No cross-route quality ranking is valid because neither subscription route returned an analysis.

The initial local start script failed because this official client rejects overriding the built-in openai provider. Private diagnostic scripts omitted those incompatible retry overrides, preserving the container/child restrictions and disabling unbounded retries. Client-default transport retries therefore remained; this is not evidence of zero provider retries or a shipped adapter fix. Both inference turns timed out with cause unresolved. Exact temporary worker/proxy containers and socket volume were removed with `docker rm --force` / `docker volume rm`; no host OAuth cache was copied. The two temporary raw upload exports were removed using verified workspace-contained literal paths; the declared preparation assets retain their 24-hour cleanup markers. Copies separately exported by the owner remain owner-controlled.

[ADR 061](adr/061-private-cloud-media-benchmark-network.md) records the approved exception and runtime gate. The cloud runtime skill explicitly requires current enforcement state enforced; unknown cannot establish readiness. No network bypass, broader grant, commercial subscription integration, unattended app dispatch, new deployment or completed V1 acceptance is claimed. Next actions are diagnosing the local incomplete-turn transport and obtaining verified current Codex cloud enforcement before another media attempt. No additional MFA or consent is currently known to be required. A native authenticated no-store request to the real production domain `/api/health` returned ok and build `0.1.0-alpha.20261005233723.g97a7caf2b553`, commit `97a7caf2b5532093568f6c51e446789e7f413def`. The official provider implementation documents this per-event marker (https://github.com/PostHog/posthog/issues/41602). Earlier test events remain historical evidence; they must not be described as having GeoIP disabled. Production receipt without GeoIP is pending the corrected build. No source text, URL or account email was present in the inspected page event.

Fresh production upload on main c5dcfd9 completed automatically at generation1 without a supplied transcript or retry. The isolated worker prepared media, the active Windows runner transcribed English audio locally, and gpt-5.6-sol medium completed three saved cited insights with full_sampled coverage. Usage was 5,182 input and 1,376 output tokens. The first full engine proof produced four insights; the independent automatic regression produced three. These counts describe actual outputs, not a fixed expected number. Private production-automatic-upload-proof.json records the source/generation/result.

Repeated clean reauthentication revealed a separate callback defect: WorkOS completed and the fresh-session query returned true, but callback307 redirected to the immutable Netlify deploy hostname; canonical domain routing then revisited consumed callback parameters and returned500. Network evidence contains paths/statuses only. The AuthKit callback now uses its supported baseURL option with the configured APP_URL, preserving PKCE/state verification. A regression, lint and types passed; production clean callback verification remains pending.

## 1 October 2026: tax evidence, insight navigation and sandbox checkout

The owner supplied a dated official ANAF query reporting absence from both ordinary Article 316 registration and cancelled-registration registers. Its exact text and query identifier are retained under ignored private/company-records/anaf-2026-10-01-owner-query.txt. Live checkout remains disabled; no professional legal review is claimed. Public legal-content copies are synchronized with the source drafts.

The refinement preserves the quiet dark design. A single named button covers each source row; library summaries clamp to three lines. Main-point links navigate to insights; transcript citations open and focus their disclosure, and private frame references open evidence. Shared buttons, disclosure summaries and standalone policy/account links use 44px minimum targets. Billing prices use responsive cards instead of a wide table, and checkout countries come from configured markets. Source image loading is lazy with explicit dimensions. Inactive subscription controls are hidden until a subscription exists.

Baseline browser measurements showed an idle workspace fetch of about 21 KB every 15 seconds. The client now refreshes every 60 seconds when idle, every 15 seconds during active processing, skips hidden/offline tabs and deduplicates overlapping refreshes. Superseded library requests are aborted; unchanged source revisions do not refetch private details. This implements a 75% lower idle polling frequency; a post-deployment network trace is still required before claiming a measured production improvement.

Checkout reservations now bind request intent. Identical retries reuse one Stripe session, while changed selections receive a safe explicit conflict until the 31-minute reservation expires. The operator-only accountant download checks active invoice permission, matching provider customer/workspace and test/live mode, limits request rate and returns actual provider totals plus seller and available buyer details. It is a private JSON handover, not an issued Oblio invoice or an ANAF receipt. ADR 014 and the Oblio guide record these boundaries.

Official Sandbox 3.5.1 replaces active E2B calls and its dependency. Customer VMs explicitly disable persistence and exposed ports, pin clean tool snapshots, use Frankfurt with no region failover, and enter independent unprivileged mount/PID/network/IPC/UTS namespaces. Root and SDK-user homes are denied; no credentials enter guest commands. The public downloader uses a bounded root-owned Unix HTTPS CONNECT broker with pinned public DNS answers. It rejects mixed/private answers, literals, non-443 ports and unapproved destinations. Decoding switches to deny-all. Artifact reads reject symlinks, hardlinks and privileged/nonregular/over-limit files. Cancellation never resumes a VM and safely repeats after deletion. ADR 023 and the runbook document operation and remaining gates.

## 2 October 2026: reviewed offline repository dependencies

ADR 030 adds a clean public-lockfile package cache, bounded public registry metadata mirror, frozen offline dependency preparation and exact source checks before/after installation. ## 3 October 2026: metered Google adapter and federation preparation

Against e578de1, implemented ADR 036's explicit Google metered route, production-only OIDC/STS impersonation broker, payload-bound atomic consumption, immutable EUR 10 operator ceiling and conservative job cost reservations. No service-account key exists; the exact production team/project subject is the only workload identity accepted. node private/google-owned-provider-probe.mjs made one tiny owned text request, with no customer content, using operator CLI authentication against the documented EU endpoint. It returned status=ok with 80 input and 5 output tokens, approximately USD 0.00004015 at the reviewed tariff. CountTokens succeeded first; the generated request included schema overhead beyond its count, so the adapter additionally reserves schema bytes and 2,048 input tokens. This proves model/endpoint support, not production federation or media quality.

The hosted media adapter automatically transcribes 32 kbit/s MP3, analyzes up to sixteen periodic/change frames in bounded batches and shares the source's ten-credit ceiling across compute, speech, vision and final extraction. ## Insight presentation and remembered Library position, October9

ADR098 implements the owner's refinement of the existing Atlas world. New completed analyses preserve their descriptive title and a whitelisted semantic icon, with a deterministic fallback for older clients. Legacy records display their existing title and a stable subject icon without rewriting claims, evidence, corrections or generations. Tree and Folders use single-line ellipsized category labels, the selected-topic heading, inline complete evidence and exact-version proposal previews. One bounded journey page per selected topic is shared by its cards; no automatic pagination, per-card query or new polling is introduced. Known proposal badges exclude rejected/deleted and observed completed drafts, and a plus denotes an additional evaluations page.

The current filing root opens by default. Browser preferences store the authorized topic, representation, filing, closed branch, expanded insight and bounded scroll geometry under separate workspace/scope keys. Fresh-scope restoration and search reset clear in-memory location and pending scroll before using the new authorized page; unavailable branches drop insight/geometry. Closing a category removes its active appearance. Nested pages select their corresponding bottom action; Save remains centered. Small metric/connection explanations use accessible circle-help disclosures.
